# Add 67 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 67 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Routine-by-routine reverse-engineering-derived conversion of all six C64 California Games events to a 32K BBC Micro Model B. Native 6502 game rules are adapted directly, with rebuilt BBC graphics, disk overlays and sound; not a game-specific emulation wrapper.
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/src/bmx_game.asm
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/Makefile
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/LICENSE
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/commit/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/tree/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0

### Classification (reviewed)

src/bmx_game.asm retains C64 address labels and adapted state-vector logic. README reports differential variable/frame tests; those claims were not reproduced. MIT for own code, tools and documentation. LICENSE explicitly excludes Epyx game designs/assets and limits permitted conversion distribution to free non-commercial use.
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/src/bmx_game.asm
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/Makefile
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/LICENSE
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/commit/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/tree/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Recovered/adapted input is the original C64 6510 game logic; output is native BBC 6502.
- https://github.com/huppy/California-Games-for-BBC-Micro/blob/071e808c180bf58c284f2e87b3edf9990ef8b0a0/src/bmx_game.asm

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable native C64 emulator for Sharp SM5A Game & Watch ROMs, supporting Ball, Vermin, Fire and Helmet through a shared CPU interpreter and per-title display adapters. Distinct from native C64 gameplay remakes.
- https://github.com/Sabbi/GNW64/blob/39c22f832e1760b21f1bb91746142345e33198d3/src/opcodefunctions.asm

Reviewed opcodefunctions.asm arithmetic/register handlers and main.asm VM state. README credits MAME adaptation. Multiple ROM targets make it reusable rather than a single-game wrapper. ### Identity (reviewed)

Gary James’s native Blitz Basic maze game, preserved and adjusted by earok. This is editable Amiga game implementation, including map, player/enemy/pill updates and IFF assets, rather than a WHDLoad slave or emulation wrapper.
- https://github.com/earok/KCMunchkin/blob/c450d757c6cdce815342859d52feb10333d515f7/kcmunchkin.bb

Substantial readable implementation: copper setup, sprite conversion, game-state loop, enemy movement and pill collision. Latest commit adds alternate graphics switching. GPL-2.0 text is included as gpl-2.0.txt; bundled Amiga system libraries and assets are not assumed to inherit that licence.

Mike Cooper’s Amiga Format competition platform game, supplied with permission and detokenized to readable Blitz Basic by the preservation repository. It has multiple playable characters, world logic and original graphics/sounds.

### Identity (reviewed)

Recovered isometric action shooter by Peter & Kalle K, originally entered in the Amiga Format Blitz Basic competition and recovered from an Acid Software hard drive. earok’s preservation adds fixes to oxygen timing, corrupt levels and end-of-game looping.

### Identity (reviewed)

Merlyn Kline’s native ClearView hypertext reader/compiler preserved with a 32-bit-clean RISC OS build path. Supports wrapped multi-font text and inline Sprites, Draw files and Ace Films; source includes reading, compilation, search, bookmarks and printing.
- https://github.com/philpem/clearview/blob/525051915570dc850612b0beb27e457b6edb74be/%21ClearGen/%21ClearView/c/Reader
- https://github.com/philpem/clearview/blob/525051915570dc850612b0beb27e457b6edb74be/%21ClearGen/%21ClearView/c/Comp

- https://github.com/philpem/clearview/commit/525051915570dc850612b0beb27e457b6edb74be

### Classification (reviewed)

Full multi-file reader/compiler implementation and native resource tree; Reader includes Wimp events, topic selection, font management and flexible-memory behavior. Apache-2.0 LICENSE for repository. README separately says bundled EuclidX remains under its original module terms; do not blanket-relicense that component.

### Identity (reviewed)

George Nakos’s Atari ST media-handling plugin and SamariTan CLI, supporting ST/MSA/DIM floppy images and AHDI/ICD-style hard disk images. Contains image decoding, FAT filesystem integration and create/extract/update operations on modern hosts.
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/dllmain.c
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/SamariTan.c
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/dosfs-1.03/dosfs.c
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/Makefile
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/build_linux_mac.sh
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/build_samaritan.sh
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/readme.md
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/commit/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial read/write media implementation and recent geometry/MSA overrun fixes. README explicitly warns about write bugs and unsupported extended XGM partitions. No clear project-wide licence found. README documents DOSFS terms and borrowed MIT SdFat / Apache-2.0 PCG code; those notices do not establish a uniform licence for all Jacknife code.
- https://github.com/ggnkua/Jacknife/blob/97ce2a49376dd8d03b283c63d3f75fa29f7b75bb/dllmain.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Atari cartridge-image creation tool by ggnkua. Parses TOS PRG inputs, writes cartridge application headers, performs relocation, and embeds a native 68000 loader that copies programs from ROM to RAM before running them.
- https://github.com/ggnkua/USM/blob/9995184bc5e565d5c49a7ba08742ee75ce6f77c1/usm.c

### Identity (reviewed)

George Nakos and Douglas Little’s linker-stage conversion utility turns m68k ELF into native Atari TOS PRG, including relocations and optional symbol tables. It is useful to cross-development workflows and preserves a dedicated Atari executable-format implementation.
- https://github.com/ggnkua/brownout-git/blob/757cee3833c16a7c8f55389965016a8d4768ec0a/brownout.cpp
- https://github.com/ggnkua/brownout-git/blob/757cee3833c16a7c8f55389965016a8d4768ec0a/buildit.sh
- https://github.com/ggnkua/brownout-git/blob/757cee3833c16a7c8f55389965016a8d4768ec0a/buildit_mingw.sh
- https://github.com/ggnkua/brownout-git/blob/757cee3833c16a7c8f55389965016a8d4768ec0a/readme.md
- https://github.com/ggnkua/brownout-git/commit/757cee3833c16a7c8f55389965016a8d4768ec0a

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial implementation handles 68k relocation types, TOS layout and symbol output; latest pinned change aligns sections to 2-byte boundaries. README/source say project code is “WTFPL. Probably.”, an imprecise declaration; dependencies have separate ELFIO/SimpleOpt licence notices. Do not present as a uniformly verified licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Eduardo Ojeda’s original 2002 two-player Classic Mac arena game and its 2026 native SDL3 restoration. Original C code and art are retained; a replacement SAT/QuickDraw compatibility layer allows the physics, ball, ships, missiles and power-ups to run on modern macOS and Windows.
- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/blob/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e/original/code/sBall.c
- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/blob/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e/src/sBall.c
- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/blob/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e/src/compat/sat.c
- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/blob/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e/Makefile

- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/commit/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e

### Classification (reviewed)

Especially strong original-source preservation and port boundary: original/code and SDL-backed compat coexist. Original ball physics and modern rendering adaptation inspected. Full latest commit trailer explicitly credits Claude Fable 5.1. MIT LICENSE, copyright Eduardo Ojeda 2002–2026. Original bundled toolkit/resources should retain applicable third-party notices.
- https://github.com/eduojeda/ascent/blob/7044620480df2b8d19ddd8db49bbfeedae22fd5e/original/code/sBall.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Tom Pickering’s C++ recreation of Fantasia’s 1997 RISC OS puzzle game using original assets with permission. Implements 100 levels over five worlds, with wizard/blob switching, ladders, falling/pushable/breakable blocks and collectables.
- https://github.com/tompickering/wizapp/blob/b2644c3f12753885a24018745a33fc5ee26ff5dc/src/character.cpp

Substantive gameplay implementation with turn, climb, fall, push and collectable logic, save system and original levels. README lists several differences/missing features from original. ### Identity (reviewed)

Stephan Kleinert’s original BBC BASIC RISC OS desktop implementation of the Robots/Daleks survival game. Retains tokenized editable BASIC source in !RunImage, desktop templates, sprites, scores, lives and teleport mechanics.
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/%21Daleks/%21RunImage%2Cffb
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/%21Daleks/%21Run%2Cfeb
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/README.md
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/LICENSE
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/commit/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd

### Classification (reviewed)

Validated the entire 27,418-byte !RunImage as a tokenized BBC BASIC program: 1,035 sequential line records (line numbers 1–1035), every length and CR boundary valid, ending exactly in CR FF with no trailing machine-code executable. Identified 64 DEF PROC declarations, every named PROC call resolved within this file, and 65 ENDPROC tokens (one extra early exit). Inspected gameplay procedures NewGame, NextLevel, DoDaleks, DoMovement and DoTeleport as source token/data records. This is recoverable program source, not incidental strings from a compiled binary. MIT LICENSE, copyright Stephan Kleinert 2019; source banner likewise explicitly calls it MIT.
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/%21Daleks/%21RunImage%2Cffb
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/%21Daleks/%21Run%2Cfeb
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/README.md
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/blob/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd/LICENSE
- https://github.com/steph72/Daleks-riscos/commit/36e5c43c329946ada14ddfddab307af2601657dd

Original homebrew RISC OS implementation, not a recovered predecessor binary. Its target is ARM RISC OS.

### Identity (reviewed)

Kochise’s archived native Atari Falcon030 Mandelbrot/Julia explorer. Full 68030/FPU assembler implementation controls the Falcon video mode, screen memory and blitter before computing fractal iterations.
- https://github.com/Kochise/atari-fractexp/blob/3c0585ea1deb759efcab5b6314b39d15e5282e19/FRACTEXP.S
- https://github.com/Kochise/atari-fractexp/blob/3c0585ea1deb759efcab5b6314b39d15e5282e19/README.md
- https://github.com/Kochise/atari-fractexp/blob/3c0585ea1deb759efcab5b6314b39d15e5282e19/LICENSE.md
- https://github.com/Kochise/atari-fractexp/commit/3c0585ea1deb759efcab5b6314b39d15e5282e19

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual assembly includes BasePage memory sizing, VSETMODE, screen allocation, blitter initialization, complex-plane window math and Mandel/Julia routines. Small but substantive hardware-specific graphics implementation. MIT LICENSE.md; original French/Atari-charset comments retained.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native 6809 recreation of the 1971 discrete-logic arcade game. Gameplay, artwork, timing and sound were measured from the companion FPGA recreation and schematics. game.asm implements missile lives, game flow, scores, collisions, explosions and hyperspace rather than emulating an original CPU.
- https://github.com/djmips/computerspace-vectrex/blob/bbe9068d0cdb9b462d610a873e439730a07f932d/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Interactive Vectrex handwriting recognizer: joystick or experimental light-pen stroke input, 12×12 integer rasterization and a 144–64–10 neural network running on the console CPU. vecnist.asm implements drawing, scaling, first-layer accumulation, second-layer multiplication and score displays; weights and training/test twins are supplied.
- https://github.com/magneato/vecnist/blob/38047e05cd3ed51d9c4f49fbda87c57514b8716c/README.md
- https://github.com/magneato/vecnist/blob/38047e05cd3ed51d9c4f49fbda87c57514b8716c/LICENSE.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Upstream reports 400 byte-exact Python-twin comparisons in MAME and 93.2% MNIST accuracy. README explicitly says real hardware and optional light pen are unverified. BSD-3-Clause-Clear (Clear BSD); includes explicit absence of patent grants.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native C89 scrolling shooter consolidating Star Rise, Bydo Incursion, Star Fall and Final Strike into one cartridge. main.c has chapter loading, ship selection, force pod, charged beam, waves, bosses and chapter-unlock flow; graphics swap by chapter to fit tile memory.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-rtype-trilogy/blob/dcd4e950305230f7138a545f588f8b64c10d2a71/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Native NGPC fan reimagining of SNK’s Last Resort with two ships, five stages, orbital option-pod control, charged wave cannon, multiple pod weapons and monochrome mode. main.c has stage terrain installation, pod movement/fire and independent game progression.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-last-resort/blob/5e8ceffe56121bc6a7985740d79405eaa1d47c53/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Shares much shooter infrastructure and even stale R-Type source headings with R-Type Neo; separate gameplay/stage/pod adaptation and standalone release justify a distinct subject, with lineage disclosed. MIT code text with inherited Asteroids notice; README additionally says no commercial use and credits sprite reference rips. Treat commercial/asset rights as unresolved rather than blanket MIT coverage.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native puzzle-fighter proof of concept with falling gem pairs, rotations, gravity, crashes, rectangular power gems, chain damage, CPU garbage attacks, story/endless modes and unlocks. src/game.c contains actual grid/phase resolution and difficulty tables; field.c renders through NGPC tile hardware.

### Identity (reviewed)

Button-driven native C reimplementation of Zork I with verb/noun selection and scrolling text. engine.c directly implements room/object accessibility, light sources, inventory, puzzles, score, troll/thief interactions and victory state; this is not a Z-machine CPU-wrapper port.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-zork-one/blob/b25ae61e0c3e824eac2cdf6a49f53e1a8e9d8d18/README.md
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-zork-one/blob/b25ae61e0c3e824eac2cdf6a49f53e1a8e9d8d18/LICENSE

- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-zork-one/commit/b25ae61e0c3e824eac2cdf6a49f53e1a8e9d8d18

### Classification (reviewed)

Pinned commit says all puzzles and working flash save, while README still describes checkpoint-one limits and unconfirmed Mednafen flash persistence. engine.c says 110 rooms/122 objects. Report inconsistent documentation; completion/playability unverified. Port LICENSE is MIT with inherited Asteroids/template notice. README claims original Zork content MIT and references LICENSE-ZORK, but that file is absent in pinned tree: original content licensing needs independent verification.

### Identity (reviewed)

C++/Qt tracker, piano-roll, instrument/SFX and project tool for native NGPC sound export. polling_driver.cpp supplies real Z80 polling-driver bytes and command host logic; project includes clean-room Z80/T6W28 preview cores and C/ASM output.
- https://github.com/Tixul/NGPCraft-Ngpc-sound-creator/blob/a79b1e657b9fb5142ab5cf54b89b9375bbf51f49/README.md

Substantial composition/export system with project workflows and driver pack. VGM import, PCM and effective stereo PAN are explicitly out of scope; outputs are tuned for supplied driver. MIT own code including first-party clean-room cores; redistributed Qt and FFmpeg remain LGPL with dynamic-linking obligations. Generated music/data belongs to creators per licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Material NGPC-targeting fork of GB Studio 4.3.2 with V3 Python compiler, native C generation, audio conversion and emulator-backed play/debug. v3Build.ts snapshots project/resources and invokes V3 export/build-ROM; compiler.py validates reachable IR and creates specialized build plans.
- https://github.com/Tixul/Ngpcraft-studio/blob/f53c293d0b5efc0a665685dc11d216121db56b2f/README.md
- https://github.com/Tixul/Ngpcraft-studio/blob/f53c293d0b5efc0a665685dc11d216121db56b2f/LICENSE

- https://github.com/Tixul/Ngpcraft-studio/commit/f53c293d0b5efc0a665685dc11d216121db56b2f

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantive alternate-target compiler/editor, not a cosmetic GB Studio fork. Work in progress; claims classify 151 events and 128 engine fields but no compatibility tests were run here. MIT GB Studio base and MIT NgpCraft additions; pinned ngpcraft-v3/LICENSE retains GBVM, startup and sound-creator notices. Proprietary Toshiba toolchain not distributed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Explicit native C/Z80 rewrite of NES Final Fantasy rules using Disch’s disassembly as specification and user-ROM extracted assets, not 6502 translation/emulation. Battle overlays, fixed-bank services, maps and quest state run through 512KB MegaCart banking with SGM RAM.
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/blob/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385/README.md
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/blob/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385/LICENSE
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/blob/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385/src/ovl_battle.c
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/blob/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385/tools/build_megacart.ps1
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/commit/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385

### Classification (reviewed)

Important partial snapshot: first-arc/bridge target, omitted saving/chests/battle actions/animations noted in README. Upstream reports 560 emulator checks, one unreproduced flaky bridge run; never real hardware; unseeded end-to-end quest not yet validated. MIT original engine/tools only; no original game data included, separate z88dk Clarified Artistic startup terms, cover excluded. Original Final Fantasy program/assets remain rights-holder property.
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/coleco-ff/blob/3c862063b035961bda97c48ac03943d0b8c8e385/README.md

Windows PowerShell 5.1 scripts, z88dk 2.4, owned US Final Fantasy ROM checked by CRC, separately obtained Disch disassembly and Coleco BIOS for MAME. Import script generates assets then build_all compiles banks/overlays into slice78_mc512.rom. ### Identity (reviewed)

Jasper de Keijzer’s 1996 five-in-a-row game, preserved as original ICC sources and a 2026 OpenWatcom Presentation Manager continuation. The 19×19 game implements position updates, computer move search and win detection; this is its own native game lineage.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-BOARD-Gomoku/blob/90f70c23f28ab2ef94b03c49a165038ac3b6db0c/src/gomoku.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Herbert Bushong’s 1996–1997 OS/2 Lights Out puzzle continued as a plain-C 32-bit Presentation Manager rewrite of its preserved Borland OWL source. The 6×8 board has two-state and three-state modes, scoring and persistent settings.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-PUZZLE-Lites_Out/blob/ba3cdc5abeb2a44863523f05b6d74c9be65e9bc5/src/litesout.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Jan M. Danielsson’s 1997 two-player worm combat game restored for OpenWatcom with native DIVE graphics, MMPM/2 audio and configurable keyboard/joystick input. Its substantial game-thread code implements worms, missiles, apple powerups and round results.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-PUZZLE-HalfWorm/blob/4329aa73efc3458492bc091619db62baf106cacd/src/GameThread.c

Dmitry Ivanov’s native OS/2 enclosure game, continued from the 2002 open-source release and modernized to OpenWatcom. The player builds walls to contain moving balls; implementation includes runner/hunter motion, ball collisions and territory replacement.

### Identity (reviewed)

Michael G. Slack’s 1997–1998 Sibyl OS/2 adaptation of Alex Lane’s 1990 minefield-walking puzzle, translated to C/OpenWatcom in 2026. The game implements mine placement, travel/marking and a new bounded safe-cell deduction solver.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-PUZZLE-Kaboooom/blob/31b50a76f83aab9507def691ad764cdaba492136/src/game.c

Michael G. Slack’s 2001 Sibyl blackjack game translated to native C/OpenWatcom for OS/2. The rules implementation covers multi-deck shuffling, dealer/player scoring, splitting, doubling and insurance, with original Pascal retained.

### Identity (reviewed)

Peter Wansch’s 1994 native Reversi game continued in OpenWatcom with preserved ICC sources. Its independent engine implements legal-move detection, disc flipping, positional evaluation and recursive multi-level computer search.

### Identity (reviewed)

Andreas Kieser’s 1996–1999 VisPro/REXX Yahtzee-style dice game rebuilt in C for native OS/2 Presentation Manager. It supports one to five players and implements category scoring, held dice, bonus calculations and score lists.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-CASINO-Kniffel/blob/3507dd5986f3736f434aafed779ed23392089183/src/kniffel.c

### Identity (reviewed)

James L. Dean’s 1988 native maze game, carried through a 2023 GCC/ArcaOS port and a 2026 OpenWatcom restoration. It generates window-sized mazes, tracks path/backtracking and computes a visible solution.

### Identity (reviewed)

Mateusz Viste’s low-requirement DOSMid music player extended by Tronix with Creative Music System/Game Blaster output and simple percussion. Real-mode C includes MIDI/MUS decoding, timing, hardware outputs and a substantial CMS voice/register implementation; this is a substantive native application branch.
- https://github.com/Tronix286/DOSMID/blob/d485a86c2edb8ebaf2112a7b9b004f9f95dff3ff/DOSMID.C
- https://github.com/Tronix286/DOSMID/blob/d485a86c2edb8ebaf2112a7b9b004f9f95dff3ff/CMS.C

CMS voice, register, frequency and percussion implementation plus main player reviewed. Fork contributes real functionality rather than only mirroring source. BSD-2-Clause-style terms for original DOSMid in README.TXT. CMS extension credits third-party snippets; bundled Creative AWE32 libraries have separate provenance and are not independently cleared by the player licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable browser player and test platform for floppy-era PC-98 freeware/doujin software. Integrates NP2kai hardware emulation with independently implemented DOS executable loading, INT 21h services, EXEC/TSR/MCB/XMS support, archive/FAT extraction and a browser UI. It accepts multiple user-supplied programs and is not a game-specific CPU wrapper.
- https://github.com/msonrm/quubee/blob/f82cc5a0e797818ffb5ea41eed5f42d43d5ed9a7/native/dos_loader.c
- https://github.com/msonrm/quubee/blob/f82cc5a0e797818ffb5ea41eed5f42d43d5ed9a7/native/dos_int21.c

Do not infer one MIT licence covers all dependencies.

### Relationships (reviewed)

Builds on NP2kai as a reusable hardware backend; QuuBee adds a substantive HLE-DOS, loader and frontend layer. Designed for circa-1998-and-earlier PC-98 freeware/doujin programs; includes headless/MCP smoke-test tooling.

Preserves Harvard’s extensible interactive PPL implementation from SAIL/TENEX, including assembly interpreter/parser/runtime modules, build/link command files, manuals and an additional archived Unix distribution.

## Research event notes

Bounded broad discovery with programmatic URL screening before pinned implementation/build/licence inspection and title/lineage deduplication. Not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
